- **thread-trace**: Diagnoses why a compute kernel on an AMD GPU is slow, instruction by instruction, with Advanced Thread Trace (rocprofv3 --att) and the rocprof-trace-decoder Python API. Use when a kernel runs below its expected throughput and you need the instructions and source lines that hold the time, such as waves stalled on busy vector or matrix (MFMA) instructions, waiting on memory (s_waitcnt) or at barriers (__syncthreads), or idle; or when capturing or analyzing .att traces. Not for hardware counters, roofline, PC sampling, host-side timelines, or NVIDIA GPUs. ([ROCm/rocm-systems](https://github.com/ROCm/rocm-systems/tree/HEAD/projects/rocprofiler-sdk/skills/thread-trace) · ★567)
